** Exciting Employment Opportunity for Advanced Practice Providers! ** 

\r\n\r\n

Join the dynamic team at CaroMont Medical Group, located just a short drive from the vibrant city of Charlotte, NC—one of the fastest-growing cities in the nation! We are seeking Board Certified Advanced Practice Providers (NP/PA) to work across our Primary Care Service Line which includes our Family Medicine and Urgent Care Clinics. 

\r\n\r\n

** Why CaroMont? ** 

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Health is an independent, not-for-profit healthcare system that prides itself on delivering exceptional care through a 476-bed hospital, brand-new satellite campus in Belmont, NC which opened its doors to the public in January 2025, extensive primary care, specialty physician network, and an accredited Cancer Center. We are deeply committed to enhancing the health and well-being of our community.  

\r\n\r\n

** What We Offer: ** 

\r\n\r\n

- Competitive Compensation and Benefits Package: Enjoy a comprehensive benefits package that includes a productivity bonus, CME, Paid Time Off, Generous Benefits, and Malpractice Coverage.   

\r\n\r\n

- Supportive administrative team and leadership  

\r\n\r\n

- Opportunities for professional development and growth  

\r\n\r\n

- Community and Lifestyle: Experience the charm of family-oriented communities with beautiful neighborhoods, top-notch public and private schools, and a strong sense of community spirit.   

\r\n\r\n

Just minutes away from the bustling Charlotte metro area, you’ll have access to fabulous shopping, gourmet dining, exciting professional sporting events, concerts, and cultural experiences. Plus, with Charlotte Douglas International Airport nearby, traveling is a breeze! 

\r\n\r\n

** Outdoor Adventures Await: ** 

\r\n\r\n

Enjoy a four-season climate with opportunities for outdoor recreation. Lakes, mountains, and beaches are all just a short drive away, perfect for those who love to explore nature and seek adventure. 

\r\n\r\n

** Ready to Join Us? ** 

\r\n\r\n

If you’re interested in bringing your skills to our team and making a positive impact in the community, we encourage you to apply today!  Visit www.caromonthealth.org to start your journey with us.  

\r\n\r\n

Don’t miss out on this incredible opportunity to build your career while enjoying a fulfilling lifestyle in a welcoming community!

We are seeking two Board-Certified Advanced Practice Providers (NP/PA) to join our new CaroMont Urgent Care and Occupational Medicine clinic in Shelby, located within our Shelby Family Medicine clinic.

\r\n\r\n

 Position Highlights

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Practice in our well-equipped facilities with access to CaroMont’s extensive resources and network.
    • \r\n\t
  • In-house X-ray capability and onsite laboratory
    • \r\n\t
  • Split or rotating coverage between Urgent Care and Occupational Medicine services
    • \r\n\t
  • 8:00 am -7:30 pm/ 7 days per week; 2-week rotating schedule
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 Key Responsibilities

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Evaluate and treat approximately 4 patients per hour with a wide range of urgent and non-emergent conditions
    • \r\n\t
  • Perform procedures such as wound care, suturing, splinting, and other urgent care services
    • \r\n\t
  • Deliver occupational medicine services, including injury care and physicals
    • \r\n\t
  • Order, interpret, and manage diagnostic tests including labs and imaging
    • \r\n\t
  • Provide education on treatment plans and follow-up care
    • \r\n\t
  • Document all encounters accurately and timely in EPIC
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 Requirements

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Master’s or Doctoral degree from accredited NP or PA program
    • \r\n\t
  • Board Certified FNP or PA-C
    • \r\n\t
  • Active NC License & DEA and Active SC License & DEA
    • \r\n\t
  • BLS/ACLS certification
    • \r\n\t
  • DOT certification required
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

Why CaroMont?

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Health is an independent, not-for-profit healthcare system that prides itself on delivering exceptional care through a 476-bed hospital, brand new satellite campus in Belmont, NC which opened its doors to the public in January 2025, extensive primary care, specialty physician network, and an accredited Cancer Center. We are deeply committed to enhancing the health and well-being of our community. 

\r\n\r\n

What We Offer:

\r\n\r\n

- Competitive Compensation and Benefits Package: Enjoy a comprehensive benefits package that includes a productivity bonus, CME, Paid Time Off, Generous Benefits, and Malpractice Coverage.  

\r\n\r\n

- Supportive administrative team and leadership 

\r\n\r\n

- Opportunities for professional development and growth 

\r\n\r\n

- Community and Lifestyle: Experience the charm of family-oriented communities with beautiful neighborhoods, top-notch public and private schools, and a strong sense of community spirit.  

\r\n\r\n

Just 45 miles away from the bustling Charlotte metro area, you’ll have access to fabulous shopping, gourmet dining, exciting professional sporting events, concerts, and cultural experiences. Plus, with Charlotte Douglas International Airport nearby, traveling is a breeze!

\r\n\r\n

 Outdoor Adventures Await:

\r\n\r\n

Enjoy a four-season climate with opportunities for outdoor recreation. Lakes, mountains, and beaches are all just a short drive away, perfect for those who love to explore nature and seek adventure.

\r\n\r\n

Ready to Join Us?

\r\n\r\n

If you’re interested in bringing your skills to our team and making a positive impact on the community, we encourage you to apply today!  Visit www.caromonthealth.org to start your journey with us.

\r\n

CaroMont Family Medicine – Stanley is seeking an experienced Advanced Practice Provider (NP or PA) to join our primary care team which consists of one MD, two APPs, and one LCSW.  This well-established practice serves a large and growing Medicare population. This opportunity offers the ability to build meaningful, long-term relationships with patients while providing comprehensive care in a supportive practice environment.

\r\n\r\n

** What We’re Looking For **

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Demonstrated clinical experience as an Advanced Practice Provider (APP) within Family Medicine.
    • \r\n\t
  • Career-oriented professional seeking a long-term practice home to foster professional growth, clinical leadership, and community impact.
    • \r\n\t
  • Dedication to establishing long-term patient relationships and ensuring continuity of care within the community.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

** Position Highlights **

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Join a collaborative and supportive team environment with a strong commitment to quality care.
    • \r\n\t
  • Practice within a modern, well-equipped facility supported by the comprehensive resources and network of CaroMont Health.
    • \r\n\t
  • Comprehensive onsite diagnostics, including laboratory services and digital X-ray capabilities.
    • \r\n\t
  • Outpatient schedule: Monday through Friday, 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM or 4 10-hour shifts.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

** Key Responsibilities **

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Order, interpret, and manage diagnostic evaluations, including laboratory tests and diagnostic imaging.
    • \r\n\t
  • Deliver patient education regarding individualized treatment plans, chronic disease management, and follow-up care.
    • \r\n\t
  • Maintain accurate, timely, and compliant electronic health record documentation utilizing EPIC.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

** Requirements **

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Master’s or Doctoral degree from an accredited NP or PA program
    • \r\n\t
  • Board Certified FNP or PA-C
    • \r\n\t
  • Active North Carolina medical license and DEA registration, with the ability and willingness to obtain South Carolina licensure and DEA registration.
    • \r\n\t
  • BLS/ACLS certification
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

** Why CaroMont? **

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Health is an independent, not-for-profit healthcare system that prides itself on delivering exceptional care through a 476-bed hospital, brand-new satellite campus in Belmont, NC which opened its doors to the public in January 2025, extensive primary care, specialty physician network, and an accredited Cancer Center. We are deeply committed to enhancing the health and well-being of our community. 

\r\n\r\n

** What We Offer: **

\r\n\r\n

- Competitive Compensation and Benefits Package: Enjoy a comprehensive benefits package that includes a productivity bonus, CME, Non-Productive Days (Paid Time Off), Generous Benefits, and Malpractice Coverage.  

\r\n\r\n

- Supportive administrative team and leadership 

\r\n\r\n

- Opportunities for professional development and growth 

\r\n\r\n

- Community and Lifestyle: Experience the charm of family-oriented communities with beautiful neighborhoods, top-notch public and private schools, and a strong sense of community spirit.  

\r\n\r\n

Just 30 miles away from the bustling Charlotte metro area, you’ll have access to fabulous shopping, gourmet dining, exciting professional sporting events, concerts, and cultural experiences. Plus, with Charlotte Douglas International Airport nearby, traveling is a breeze!

\r\n\r\n

** Outdoor Adventures Await: **

\r\n\r\n

Enjoy a four-season climate with opportunities for outdoor recreation. Lakes, mountains, and beaches are all just a short drive away, perfect for those who love to explore nature and seek adventure.

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Family Medicine – Lake Wylie is seeking an experienced Advanced Practice Provider (NP or PA) to join our primary care team which consists of three MDs, and two APPs.  This opportunity offers the ability to build meaningful, long-term relationships with patients while providing comprehensive care in a supportive practice environment.

\r\n\r\n

** What We’re Looking For **

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Demonstrated clinical experience as an Advanced Practice Provider (APP) within Family Medicine.
    • \r\n\t
  • Career-oriented professional seeking a long-term practice home to foster professional growth, clinical leadership, and community impact.
    • \r\n\t
  • Dedication to establishing long-term patient relationships and ensuring continuity of care within the community.
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

** Position Highlights **

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Join a collaborative and supportive team environment with a strong commitment to quality care.
    • \r\n\t
  • Practice within a modern, well-equipped facility supported by the comprehensive resources and network of CaroMont Health.
    • \r\n\t
  • Comprehensive on-site diagnostics, including laboratory services and digital X-ray capabilities.
    • \r\n\t
  • Outpatient schedule: 7:30 AM to 5:00 PM
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t

    • \r\n\t
  • Maintain accurate, timely, and compliant electronic health record documentation utilizing EPIC.
    • \r\n

    • \r\n\t

  • BLS/ACLS certification

** Why CaroMont? **

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Health is an independent, not-for-profit healthcare system that prides itself on delivering exceptional care through a 476-bed hospital, brand new satellite campus in Belmont, NC which opened its doors to the public in January 2025, extensive primary care, specialty physician network, and an accredited Cancer Center. We are deeply committed to enhancing the health and well-being of our community. 

\r\n\r\n

** What We Offer: **

\r\n\r\n

Just 20 miles away from the bustling Charlotte metro area, you’ll have access to fabulous shopping, gourmet dining, exciting professional sporting events, concerts, and cultural experiences. Plus, with Charlotte Douglas International Airport nearby, traveling is a breeze!

\r\n

Sign On Bonus Available up to $13,000 (based on experience)!

\r\n\r\n

Job Summary:  To perform diversified duties in the Electrophysiology Lab and Hybrid OR, assisting the electrophysiologist intra-procedurally including preparation and positioning of patients, operation of x-ray equipment in radiological and fluoroscopic procedures, scrubbing, circulating, monitoring, and recording hemodynamic parameters according to policy. Must successfully complete corporate and department specific skill validations and maintain core competencies.

\r\n\r\n

Core Competencies:    The RN will be competent to monitor, circulate, and scrub diagnostic and interventional electrophysiology studies, ablations, and device cases such as: generator changes, PPM and ICD implants including complex CRM devices, device explants, lead extraction, venogram, temporary PPM, left atrial appendage closure, implantable loop recorders, and operation of cardiac stimulator. Responsibilities may also include training new or existing employees.  The RN Staff may progress to RN Intermediate or RN Advanced by completing skill validations and maintaining competencies to monitor, circulate, and scrub additional EP Lab procedures.

\r\n\r\n

** See EP Lab Career Advancement Ladder document for additional details

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Work Schedule & Case Coverage Expectations: EP Lab team members are not included in the STEMI on-call rotation. However, to support inpatient add-on electrophysiology cases, a designated late team is scheduled Monday through Friday. These extended shifts are assigned in advance and rotated equitably among staff to ensure fair distribution and predictability. We strive to maintain a collaborative environment where scheduling reflects both operational needs and staff wellbeing. Flexibility and teamwork are key to delivering exceptional patient care in a dynamic procedural setting.

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications:   Able to communicate and collaborate effectively with staff, patients, physicians, and families to achieve positive outcomes. Current RN license to practice in NC (NC licensure or multi-state (compact) license required).  BLS/ACLS certification required.  Experience in an EP Lab, Cardiac Cath Lab, Interventional Radiology or other procedural setting, critical care, ED, telemetry, or other cardiac nursing background is preferred. CEPS or RCES certification preferred.  

\r\n\r\n

Applicants with 2 or more years of experience in an EP Lab may be considered for the RN Intermediate skill level. Applicants with 3 or more years of experience in an EP Lab may be considered for the RN Advanced skill level. Applicant must successfully complete the corresponding competency requirements and skill validations within 90 days of hire to maintain the RN Intermediate or RN Advanced skill level.

\r\n\r\n

Job Summary:  Under the guidance and supervision of the Principal Investigator (PI), the Oncology Research Nurse is responsible for the management of oncology clinical trials, ensuring trials are conducted in accordance with federal, state, and local regulations, Institutional Review Board (IRB) approvals, and CaroMont policies. Required to cross-train in other department areas and provide back-up as needed.  Maintain confidentiality, HIPAA compliance and ensure protection of participants enrolled in clinical trials.  Ensure that scientific integrity is maintained through data reliability and strict adherence to regulatory mandates.  Act as alternate for COC and NAPBC certification meetings. Attend Tumor Board, Breast Conference, Breast Clinic, and Southeast Clinical Oncology Research Consortium (SCOR) meetings and trainings.  Other duties in support of Research Administration as assigned.

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications: Current RN license to practice in NC (NC license or multi-state compact license).  A minimum of two (2) years of nursing experience required, preferably oncology experience. BSN preferred.  Research experience strongly preferred.  BLS required;.  OCN Certification and/or ONS Chemotherapy and Biotherapy course certification preferred.   Research certification preferred, or required within (3) years of employment (e.g. SoCRA or ACRP).  Strong verbal and written communication skills, critical thinking skills, computer skills (Word, Excel), , organizational skills and problem-solving skills required.  Ability to work autonomously and in teams as required.  Flexible work hours related to various project needs may be required.  Periodic travel required (<5%).

Sign On Bonus Available up to $13,000 (based on experience)!

Job Summary:  To perform diversified duties in the Cardiac Cath Lab and Hybrid OR, assisting the cardiologist intra-procedurally including preparation and positioning of patients, operation of x-ray equipment in radiological and fluoroscopic procedures, scrubbing, circulating, monitoring, and recording hemodynamic parameters according to policy. Must successfully complete corporate and department specific skill validations and maintain core competencies.  

\r\n\r\n

Core Competencies:  The RN will be competent to monitor and circulate diagnostic and interventional cardiac catheterizations, intermediate and complex interventional cardiac procedures, STEMI, diagnostic and interventional arteriograms. Responsibilities also include STEMI on-call team coverage and precepting new or existing employees.  The RN Staff may progress to RN Intermediate by completing skill validations and maintaining competencies to monitor and circulate 2 of 5 Additional Specialty Areas. The RN Staff may progress to RN Advanced by completing skill validations and maintaining competencies to monitor and scrub 4 of 5 Additional Specialty Areas.

\r\n\r\n

Additional Specialty Areas**:

  5. CRM Devices

** See CCL/EPL Skill Based Compensation Model document for additional details

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications:   Able to communicate and collaborate effectively with staff, patients, physicians, and families to achieve positive outcomes. Current RN license to practice in NC (NC licensure or multi-state (compact) license required.  BLS/ACLS certification required.  Experience in Cardiac Cath Lab, EP Lab, special procedural settings, or critical care preferred.  Applicants with 2 or more years of experience in a Cardiac Cath Lab or EP Lab may be considered for the RN Intermediate skill level. Applicants with 3 or more years of experience in a Cardiac Cath Lab or EP Lab may be considered for the RN Advanced skill level. Applicant must successfully complete the corresponding competency requirements and skill validations within 90 days of hire to maintain the RN Intermediate or RN Advanced skill level.

  • The Nurse Educator (RN) integrates evidence-based practice into program development.
    • \r\n\t
  •  Responsible for outcomes of education programs.

  • Collaborates with approved nursing colleges and universities for departmental/unit clinical experiences.

At CaroMont Health, we believe the cure for “common medicine” is uncommon compassion. We know that the most powerful aspect of healthcare isn’t just advanced technology or even remarkable, breakthrough treatments – it’s the dedicated people who use those tools to help change and save lives.

\r\n\r\n

SIGN ON BONUS!

\r\n\r\n

Job Summary:  To render professional nursing care to a group of assigned patients within the hospital.  Collaborates with the medical staff and other members of the health care team to provide quality patient care.  Performs all activities in accordance with approved policies and guides.  Supervises and delegates appropriate duties to ancillary personnel. 

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications: 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Current RN license to practice in NC (NC licensure or multi-state (compact) license).
    • \r\n\t
  • BSN preferred; professional nursing certification preferred.  
    • \r\n\t
  • Recent acute care experience within the past 3 years is preferred. 
    • \r\n\t
  • Bilingual preferred.  Meets general hospital and departmental competency requirements. 
    • \r\n\t
  • Current technical certificates such as Basic Life Support (BLS), Advanced Cardiac Life Support (ACLS), Pediatric Advanced Life Support (PALS), NIH Stroke Scale Certification, Neonatal Resuscitation Program (NRP), STABLE or Trauma Nursing Core Course (TNCC) as required by specialty area. 
    • \r\n

Sign on Bonus Available!

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Health believe in treating the whole person, and we work closely with each patient to develop a care plan that’s individualized and effective. Our compassionate, experienced interdisciplinary team strives to provide extraordinary care as we partner with patients to help them achieve their mental wellness goals. We celebrate victories—big and small—with patients along the way. Because we believe individuals can create positive change in their lives, our approach is centered on helping patients attain and maintain optimal mental wellness.

Registered Nurse – Medical/Surgical ICU (MSICU)

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Regional Medical Center | Level III Trauma Center

\r\n\r\n

Full Time | 7a–7p or 7p–7a

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

MSICU – Medical/Surgical Intensive Care Unit

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Sign-On Bonus up to $13,000 (based on experience)

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Step into the heart of critical care at CaroMont Regional Medical Center’s Medical/Surgical Intensive Care Unit (MSICU). As part of a Level III Trauma Center, our MSICU team is dedicated to delivering compassionate, exceptional, and highly reliable care to our community.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Why Join Us?

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

High-Impact Work: Care for complex Surgical, Trauma, Medical, and Neurologic patients who need advanced monitoring, mechanical ventilation, and specialized therapies.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Dynamic Environment: Be challenged and grow in a 26-bed unit where every day brings new opportunities to sharpen your clinical expertise.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Supportive Culture: Join a team built on trust, collaboration, and shared commitment to excellence. Our values guide not only how we care for patients but also how we support each other.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Competitive Benefits: Full-time benefits package including medical, dental, vision, 403(b), paid time off, and tuition assistance.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

What You’ll Do

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

As an MSICU RN, you’ll be on the front lines of critical care—coordinating complex treatment plans, advocating for patients and families, and working closely with physicians and multidisciplinary teams to deliver life-saving care. You’ll have the chance to grow as a leader while making a meaningful difference every shift.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  • Current RN license in NC (or compact license); BSN preferred
    • \r\n\t
  • 2+ years of progressive acute care or ICU experience preferred
    • \r\n\t
  • Strong clinical knowledge and critical thinking skills
    • \r\n\t
  • Ability to thrive in a fast-paced environment, communicate effectively, and support both patients and colleagues
    • \r\n
\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

CaroMont Health’s Mission: To provide compassionate, exceptional, and highly reliable care.

\r\n\r\n

Our Vision: To be the community’s most trusted healthcare partner.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Join a team where your skills are valued, your growth is supported, and your work truly matters.

\r\n\r\n

Sign On Bonus up to $13,000 (based on experience)

\r\n\r\n

Job Summary: To render professional nursing care to a group of assigned patients within the hospital. Collaborates with the medical staff and other members of the health care team to provide quality patient care. Performs all activities in accordance with approved policies and guides. \r\n\r\n

Recent acute care experience within past 3 years preferred.  Critical Care experience strongly preferred.  Meets general hospital and departmental competency requirements.  Current BLS, ACLS and/or PALS as required by specialty.

Sign on Bonus up to $5,000 based on experience

\r\n\r\n

Job Summary:  The Imaging RN plays a critical role in supporting patients undergoing diagnostic and interventional radiology procedures. This position requires clinical expertise, adaptability, and strong communication skills to ensure safe, efficient, and compassionate care in a fast-paced, multidisciplinary environment. Responsibilities include patient assessment, procedural preparation and recovery, intraprocedural and sedation monitoring, and coordination with radiologists, technologists, and other care team members. Imaging RNs also provide on-call coverage for after-hours emergent procedures, ensuring timely response and continuity of care for patients requiring urgent imaging interventions.

\r\n\r\n

The role requires proactive engagement in pre/intra/post-procedural patient safety protocols, emergency response readiness, and adherence to departmental policies. Imaging RNs contribute to workflow optimization, patient education, and continuous quality improvement initiatives within the imaging department.

\r\n\r\n

 

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications:  Current RN license to practice in NC (NC license or multi-state (compact) license). BSN preferred; professional specialty nursing certification preferred.  Meets general hospital and departmental orientation and competency requirements.  BLS, ACLS, and PALS required. If new hires do not have current certifications, they may obtain ACLS and PALS during departmental orientation as part of their onboarding. Critical care or procedural nursing experience preferred. Must possess strong clinical assessment skills and be able to respond to emergent situations. Must be able to effectively communicate and collaborate across multidisciplinary teams.

\r\n\r\n

Job Summary:  To render professional nursing care to a group of assigned patients within the hospital.  Collaborates with the medical staff and other members of the health care team to provide quality patient care. Performs all activities in accordance with approved policies and guides. Supervises and delegates appropriate duties to   ancillary personnel. 

\r\n\r\n

Qualifications:  Current RN license to practice in NC (NC licensure or multi-state (compact) license). BSN preferred; professional nursing certification preferred. Recent acute care experience within past 3 years preferred.  Meets general hospital and departmental competency requirements. Current technical certificates such as Basic Life Support (BLS), Advanced Cardiac Life Support (ACLS) , Pediatric Advanced Life Support (PALS), NIH Stroke Scale Certification, Neonatal Resuscitation Program (NRP), STABLE or Trauma Nursing Core Course (TNCC) as required by specialty area.  

\r\n\r\n

Recent acute care experience within past 3 years preferred.  Meets general hospital and departmental competency requirements. Current technical certificates such as Basic Life Support (BLS), Advanced Cardiac Life Support (ACLS) , Pediatric Advanced Life Support (PALS), NIH Stroke Scale Certification, Neonatal Resuscitation Program (NRP), STABLE or Trauma Nursing Core Course (TNCC) as required by specialty area.
